payaṉpaṭuttiyarit tavira tiṭṭamāṉa karuttukkaḷ etiyum avar muṉvikkavilli. ākavē
īḻattil ivarāl maṭṭumē muṉvikkappaṭṭa oru ilakkiyak kōcamāka atu maṟintatu. ciṟanta
paṭippāḷiyāṉa es.po. cila nalla vimarcaṉak kaṭṭurikaḷum eḻutiyavar. eṉiṉum
ākkappūrvamāṉa vimarcaṉaṅkaḷi viṭak kaṇṭaṉaṅkaḷilēyē ivar atika caktiyic celaviṭṭuḷḷar.
pantanūl mūlamum uriyum eṉṟa ivaratu nūl ivaratu vimarcaṉa āḷumi virayamākkappaṭṭataṟkuc
ciṟanta utāraṇamākum.
mu. taḷiyaciṅkam pirapañca yatārtta vātam eṉṟa koḷkiyi muṉvittār. muṟpōkku
ilakkiyattukku etirāka kāttiramāṉa vimarcaṉak karuttukkaḷi muṉvittavar ivarē.
muṟpōkku ilakkiyattiṉ cittānta taḷamāṉa mārksīya tattuvattiyē ivar vimarcaṉattukku
uṭpaṭuttiṉār. intiyāvil mārksīyam eṉṟa collik kēṭṭālē āttiramuṟṟu avastippaṭum
veṅkaṭ cāmi nātaṉ pōl aṉṟi, mu. taḷiyaciṅkam mārksīyatti oru tattuvārtta camayavaḻi
niṉṟu vimarcittār. mārksīyattiṉ cila pakutikaḷi ēṟṟukkoṇṭu ataṟku appāl cella
vēṇṭum eṉpatē ivaratu karuttākum.
"mārksīyatti maṟukkum tuivaṟṟa kaliñarkaḷum cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷum mutalil ati uṇarntu
oppuk koṇṭāltāṉ avarkaḷiṉ maṟuppu veṟum maṟuppākavē niṉṟuviṭāmal iṉṟu cāittiram
kāṭṭiniṟkum putiya taruṇatti iṉam kaṇṭu payaṉpaṭutti mārksīyattiyum mīṟi vaḷarum
veṟṟiyāka māṟuvataṟku vaḻipiṟakkum. atāvatu anta uṇmiyi oppuk koṇṭāltāṉ anta
uṇmiyiṉ aṭutta pakkattiyum uṇaralām" eṉṟu taḷiyaciṅkam eḻutiyuḷḷār. mēlittēya
mārksīya etirppuc cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷiyum aravintar pōṉṟavarkaḷ piratipalitta
intiya āṉmika vātattiyum oṉṟiṇittatē ivaratu koḷkiyākum. avar tamatu ilakkiyak
kōṭpāṭu paṟṟi eḻutukiyil 'iṉi varavēṇṭiya kōṭpāṭṭi 'pirañca yatārttam' eṉṟu
kūṟalām; viññāṉamum āṉmika ñāṉamum, vāḻkki ākiyavaṟṟuk kiṭiyē pētam irukkātu.
vāḻkkiyē kaliyākum. kaliyi aḻikkum kali ilakkiyatti aḻikkum ilakkiyam iviyē
iṉit tōṉṟap pōkiṉṟaṉa. atuvē meyyuḷ eṉpatu ivaratu kali ilakkiyam paṟṟiya
karuttukkaḷiṉ cāramākum, antavakiyil ivar tarkka muraṇ niṟinta āṉmikak
kaṟpaṉāvātiyēyāvār. mu.poṉṉampalam, eṉ.kē. makāliṅkam, imiyavaṉ mutaliyōr ivaratu
karuttukkaḷiṉ celvākku uṭpaṭṭavarkaḷāvar, taḷiyaciṅkattiṉ pōrppaṟi, meyyuḷ ākiyavi
īḻattiṉ oru kuṟippiṭṭa ilakkiyac cintaṉiyi vuikkāṭṭum mika mukkiyamāṉa
nūlkaḷākum. ivaratu ēḻāṇṭu ilakkiya vaḷarcci - avacarak kuṟippukkaḷ eṉṟa kaṭṭurit
toṭarum kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
ituvari nām nōkkiyatil iruntu 1960 ām āṇṭukaḷil ilakkiya vimarcaṉak koḷkikaḷ
īḻattil cittānta āītiyil vaḷarcciyuṟṟaṉa eṉpatik kāṇalām. muṟpōkku ilakkiyam, tēciya
ilakkiyam eṉṟa kōṭpāṭukaḷ kāttiramāka vaḷarcciyuṟṟatāl araciyal āītiyil ati
etirttavarkaḷum cāiyākavō, piḻiyākavō tavirkka muṭiyāmal putiya kōṭpāṭukaḷiyē
muṉvikka vēṇṭi iruntatu. ataṉ viḷivē naṟpōkku vātam, pirapañca yatārttam vātam
eṉpaṉavākum.

5
ilakkiyattiṉ camutāya uḷḷaṭakkam paṟṟiya kōṭpāṭukaḷum aṇuku muṟikaḷum iṅku
vaḷarcciyaṭintatu pōl ilakkiyattiṉ uruvam paṟṟiya vimarcaṉak kōṭpāṭukaḷum īḻattil
ikkālap pakutiyil vaḷarcciyaṭintaṉa. ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ, ilakkiyap pākupāṭu, ilakkiya
racaṉi eṉpavi paṟṟiya cintaṉikaḷi ivi uḷḷaṭakkiṉa. ivvakiyil kavitik kōṭpāṭukaḷi
vakuttuk kūṟum murukiyaṉiṉ oru cila viti ceyvōm, murukiyaṉum, kilācapatiyum
eḻutiya kaviti nayam, ākiya nūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭat takkaṉa. kilācapatiyiṉ ilakkiyamum
tiṉāyvum eṉṉum nūlum ippiāivil aṭaṅkakkūṭiyatē. puṉikati vaṭivam paṟṟiya
civattampiyiṉ puṉikatiyum katippiṉṉalum eṉṉum kaṭṭuriyum kaviti nāṭakam paṟṟi
em.ē. nuḵmāṉ, mu. poṉṉampalam ākiyōr eḻutiya kaṭṭurikaḷum, putuk kavitiyiṉ
amippu paṟṟi ciṟipati, capā jeyarācā ākiyōr eḻutiya cila kaṭṭurikaḷum ilakkiya
vaṭivaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippiṭattakka kaṭṭurikaḷākum
ilakkiya vimarcaṉam, ilakkiyak koḷki, ilakkiya varalāṟu ākiyavi taṉittaṉit tuṟikaḷ
eṉiṉum avi muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭavi alla. oṉṟi oṉṟu cārntum uḷḷaṭakkiyum celvaṉa.
avvakiyil vimarcaṉa pūrvamāṉa ilakkiya varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ cilavum iṅku tōṉṟiṉa.
ivvakiyil pērāciāiyar vi. celvanāyakattiṉ tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu palarukku
ātarcamākavum muṉṉōṭiyākavum amintatu. kalāniti ā. vēluppiḷḷiyiṉ tamiḻ
ilakkiyattiṉ kālamum ilakkiyattiṉ īḻattaṟiñar peru muyaṟcikaḷ, kaṉaka centinātaṉiṉ
īḻattu ilakkiya vaḷarcci, cokkaṉiṉ īḻattu nāṭaka ilakkiya vaḷarcci, cuppiramaṇiya aiyāiṉ
īḻattut tamiḻ nāval ilakkiyam mutaliya nūlkaḷ ivvakiyil kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa.
taṉippaṭṭa paṭippukkaḷ paṭippāḷikaḷ paṟṟi vimarcaṉa pūrvamāṉa matippīṭukaḷ iṅku perumaḷavu
ceyyappaṭavilli eṉiṉum kuṟippiṭattakka cila muyaṟcikaḷ naṭipeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. kilācapatiyiṉ
iru makākavikaḷ, civattampiyiṉ tamiḻc ciṟukatiyiṉ tōṟṟamum vaḷarcciyum,
tillinātaṉiṉ vaḷḷuvar mutal pāratitācaṉ vari, cempiyaṉ celvaṉiṉ īḻattut tamiḻ
ciṟukati maṇikaḷ ākiya nūlkaḷ ivvakiyil kuṟippiṭat takkaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cempiyaṉ celvaṉiṉ
nūlē muṟṟilum īḻattup paṭippāḷikaḷ paṟṟiyatu. ivvakiyil mahākavi paṟṟi caṇmukam
civaliṅkam, em.ē. nuḵmāṉ ākiyōr eḻutiya kaṭṭurikaḷum kuṟippiṭat takkaṉa.
taṉippaṭippu eṉṟa vakiyil mahākaviyiṉ cātāraṇa maṉitaṉatu cāittiram paṟṟi caṇmukam
civaliṅkam eḻutiya viāivāṉa vimarcaṉam īḻattu vimarcaṉa muyaṟcikaḷuḷ mika mukkiyamāṉa
kavaṉattukkuāiya oṉṟākum. ivitavira īḻattil, ilakkiya vimarcaṉattuṟiyil ā.
civanēcaccelvaṉ, kalā paramēsvaraṉ, cittiralēkā, maṉōṉmaṇi caṇmukatās, mu.
nittiyāṉantaṉ, ce. yōkarācā, turi maṉōkaraṉ, ka.caṇmukaliṅkam, es.em.jē. pistīṉ,
kē.es. civakumāraṉ, em.ec.em. cams, ci. muṉakuru mutaliyōrum kuṟippiṭattakka
muyaṟcikaḷ ceytuḷḷaṉar.
camīpa kālamāka īḻattu ilakkiya vimarcaṉa muyaṟcikaḷil moḻiyil aṟiviṉ celvākkiyum kāṇa
muṭikiṉṟatu, ilakkiyam moḻiyiṉāl ākum oru kali eṉṟa vakiyil moḻiyil aṟivu
ilakkiya vimarcaṉattil naṉku payaṉpaṭa muṭiyum. mēlit tēyaṅkaḷil ittuṟi naṉku
vaḷarcciyaṭintuḷḷatu. tamiḻakattil aṇmiyil vuivanta muttuccaṇmukaṉiṉ ilakkiyak
kōṭpāṭu eṉṟa nūlil moḻiyil aṟivu naṉku payaṉpaṭṭuḷḷatik kāṇalām. īḻattil kalāniti
caṇmukatās, em.ē.nuḵmāṉ ākiyōr ittuṟiyil ciṟu muyaṟcikaḷ ceytuḷḷaṉar.
caṇmukatāsiṉ ākka ilakkiyamum moḻiyiyalum, kaviñarum moḻiyum, em.ē. nuḵmāṉiṉ ākka
ilakkiyamum naṭiyiyalum, īḻattu nāvalkaḷiṉ moḻi mutaliya kaṭṭurikaḷ ittuṟiyil ārampa
muyaṟcikaḷākak karutattakkaṉa.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

piṉṉiṇippu

īḻattu ilakkiya varalāṟṟukku utavum nūlkaḷum ciṟappitaḻkaḷum
1. nūlkaḷ

2. ciṟappitaḻkaḷ

** irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu īḻattut tamiḻ ilakkiyam ** muṟṟum.
